*Commander (2021 Edition)* releases alongside *Strixhaven: School of Mages* on April 23! Check out all the newest cards in the [*Commander (2021 Edition)* Card Image Gallery](/en/articles/card-image-gallery/2021/04-10-commander-2021-edition), as well as [the extended-art versions of *Commander (2021 Edition)* cards available in Collector Boosters](/en/articles/feature/2021/03-25-collecting-strixhaven-school-of-mages) over in the [*Strixhaven: School of Mages* Variants Card Image Gallery](/en/articles/card-image-gallery/2021/04-10-strixhaven-school-of-mages-variants).

Below, you'll find each deck, added day by day, and a link to where it was previewed with details about how the deck plays out!

***(Editor's Note: The decklists below pull the newest printing of each card automatically from*** [***Gatherer***](https://web.archive.org/web/20210405000000/https://gatherer.wizards.com/Pages/Default.aspx)***, including printings which are not in this product. These decklists are not card-for-card product displays but rather interactive lists of the cards included in each deck. Additionally, card images for new* Commander (2021 Edition) *cards will appear in decklists at a later time.)***
